### [1] [AdaPilot: Towards Scene-Adaptive Policy Learning for Cross-Generator Text-to-Image Quality Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29517)
*Wenjin Liu,Fayuan Ke,Yue Lu,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaPilot 通过将多轮图像生成建模为 MDP 并端到端强化学习，学习场景自适应、可跨生成器迁移的通用质量优化策略，在生成质量与零样本跨生成器泛化上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升文本到图像生成质量的方法（从生成器微调、提示词优化到基于多轮视觉反馈的强化学习）与特定生成器和任务深度耦合，所学习的能力难以泛化为通用的质量优化策略。

Method: 将多轮图像生成建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并通过端到端强化学习优化。具体包括三点设计：(1) 将策略与生成器内部实现解耦，以实现跨生成器迁移；(2) 引入场景感知奖励，使质量评估维度与任务语义自适应对齐；(3) 采用过程级奖励（process-level rewards）建模图像质量的演化轨迹。

Result: 实验表明 AdaPilot 在生成质量和泛化性上优于基线方法；独立的跨生成器评估显示，单一策略可零样本迁移到未见过的生成器，并在所有评估的生成器上均保持正向的平均增益。

Conclusion: AdaPilot 为文本到图像生成质量优化提供了一种与生成器解耦、可跨生成器零样本迁移的通用策略，项目代码已开源（https://github.com/QwenQing/Ada_pilot）。

Abstract: Existing methods for improving text-to-image generation quality have progressed from generator fine-tuning and prompt optimization to reinforcement learning with multi-turn visual feedback. However, existing strategies are deeply coupled with specific generators and tasks, and the learned capabilities are difficult to generalize into a universal quality optimization policy. Therefore, we propose AdaPilot, which learns a scene-adaptive, cross-generator transferable quality optimization policy by formulating multi-turn image generation as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and optimizing it via end-to-end reinforcement learning. Specifically, AdaPilot decouples the policy from generator internals to enable cross-generator transfer, introduces scene-aware rewards that adaptively align quality assessment dimensions with task semantics, and employs process-level rewards to model the evolution trajectory of image quality. Experimental results show AdaPilot outperforms baselines in generation quality and generalization. Separate cross-generator evaluations further show that a single policy transfers zero-shot to unseen generators while maintaining positive average gains across all evaluated generators. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQing/Ada_pilot.

</details>


### [2] [Albireo: Adaptive, Energy-Efficient Inference Framework for Video Object Detection on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29648)
*Amir Taherin,José Cano,Bin Ren,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Albireo 是一种与检测器无关、无需编解码信息的自适应推理框架，通过每目标 Kalman 滤波预测不确定性来决定是否调用检测器，从而在边缘设备视频目标检测中同时降低能耗并保持甚至提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上对长视频流逐帧运行高成本检测器会导致高能耗和持续 GPU 占用；虽然连续帧高度冗余，但朴素跳帧是内容盲的，会在目标进入、遮挡恢复和剧烈运动等关键帧跳过，损害检测质量。

Method: Albireo 包装现成检测器，无需修改或重训练。它为每个活跃目标维护 10 维 Kalman 滤波器（KF），仅当预测不确定性超过阈值时才调用检测器；被跳过帧直接由 KF 状态预测框，GPU 开销接近零。另含 KF 救援机制以在短暂漏检时保留已确认目标，避免输出碎片化；并用轻量空场景筛查避免无目标帧的检测调用。

Result: 在 BDD100K MOT 验证集上，使用 YOLO11x、YOLO26x、RF-DETR-Large 三种不同架构检测器，以及 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor 和 AGX Orin 两个平台评估。所有配置下，Albireo 将 AP@50 保持在逐帧推理的 ±1.2 个百分点内，同时降低总能耗 12.1%–17.6%。在 YOLO26x 上，AP@50 提升 +0.8 个百分点，能耗降低 17.6%（Thor）和 14.4%（Orin），每帧能量-延迟积分别降低 24.9% 和 26.1%。默认工作点同时改善精度、能耗和延迟；而固定间隔 50% 跳帧基线 FixedSkip-2 损失 8.6 个百分点 AP@50。

Conclusion: 基于目标级时序状态的自适应检测调用可以在边缘视频目标检测中优于内容盲跳帧，在保持检测精度甚至略有提升的同时显著降低能耗与能量-延迟积，且具备检测器无关、无需重训练的通用性。

Abstract: Video object detection on edge devices runs computationally expensive detectors over long frame streams, causing high energy consumption and sustained GPU utilization. Although consecutive frames are highly redundant, naive frame skipping is content-blind: it skips during critical moments such as object entry, occlusion recovery, and abrupt motion, degrading detection quality. We present Albireo, a detector-agnostic, codec-free adaptive inference framework that wraps off-the-shelf detectors and decides when detector invocation can be safely skipped based on scene content and per-object temporal state, requiring no detector modification or retraining. Albireo maintains a 10-dimensional Kalman filter (KF) per active object and invokes the detector only when prediction uncertainty exceeds a threshold; on skipped frames, boxes are predicted from the KF state at near-zero GPU cost. A KF-based rescue mechanism preserves confirmed objects through brief detector misses to prevent output fragmentation, while a lightweight empty-scene screen avoids detector calls on objectless frames. We evaluate Albireo on the BDD100K MOT validation split with three architecturally distinct detectors (YOLO11x, YOLO26x, RF-DETR-Large) on two NVIDIA Jetson platforms (AGX Thor, AGX Orin). Across all configurations, Albireo keeps AP@50 within +/-1.2 pp of per-frame inference while reducing total energy by 12.1-17.6%. On YOLO26x, it improves AP@50 by +0.8 pp while reducing energy by 17.6% (Thor) and 14.4% (Orin) and per-frame energy-delay product by 24.9% and 26.1%, respectively. Thus, the default operating point improves accuracy, energy, and latency together. In contrast, FixedSkip-2, a fixed-interval baseline with a 50% skip rate, loses 8.6 pp AP@50. Source code, evaluation pipeline, and per-clip results are available at https://github.com/amirtaherin/albireo

</details>


### [3] [TrackEverything: Long Horizon Dense Tracking via De-Duplicating 3D Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30222)
*Ayush Jain,Sreeharsha Paruchuri,Ishita Gupta,Fan Zhang,Tanner Schmidt,Jakob Engel,Katerina Fragkiadaki,Adam W. Harley*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrackEverything 是一种 3D 点跟踪器，通过将视频表示为世界坐标系下的持久 3D 场景轨迹，打破了“稀疏点长时跟踪”与“稠密点短片段跟踪”之间的权衡，可在 40GB GPU 显存内跟踪超过 1000 帧视频中的所有可见点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频点跟踪模型存在根本性权衡：要么在长时序上跟踪稀疏查询点，要么仅在短片段内跟踪所有点，难以兼顾长时程与全帧稠密跟踪。作者基于“视频是底层 3D 世界的 2D 投影”这一洞察，希望将模型复杂度与视频时长解耦，使其随场景中独特物理几何的规模扩展，而非随时长线性增长。

Method: 方法核心是将视频建模为世界坐标下的持久 3D 场景轨迹，并引入三项创新：1）在滑动窗口边界采用基于体素化的去重机制，合并共位轨迹，避免同一表面被重复观测而冗余累积；2）将跟踪分解为端点精化器与轻量轨迹精化器：前者预测每个点的终点并区分静态/动态，后者仅对动态点解码稠密轨迹；3）提出 3D WAFT，用场景点云中的高效特征采样替代显存开销巨大的 4D 相关体。

Result: TrackEverything 是首个能在 40GB GPU 显存内跟踪超过 1000 帧视频中所有可见点的 3D 跟踪器。在 TAPVid-3D 上，短片段中其 APD 比所有开源全帧稠密 3D 跟踪器高出 20% 以上；在长序列上，尽管跟踪点数远多于稀疏跟踪器，仍与最先进的稀疏跟踪器保持竞争力。

Conclusion: TrackEverything 通过世界坐标系下的持久 3D 场景轨迹表示，有效打破了长时程稀疏跟踪与短片段稠密跟踪之间的权衡，并借助去重、动静分解与高效特征采样实现可扩展的全点 3D 跟踪，在短片段稠密跟踪和长序列跟踪中均表现优异。

Abstract: Existing point tracking models face a fundamental tradeoff: they can either track a sparse set of query points over long horizons, or track all points across only short clips. We introduce TrackEverything, a 3D point tracker that breaks this trade-off by representing videos as persistent 3D scene tracks in world coordinates. Grounded in the insight that videos are 2D projections of an underlying 3D world, TrackEverything decouples model complexity from video duration, allowing it to scale with unique physical scene geometry instead. Our approach introduces three key innovations. First, we employ a voxelization-based de-duplication mechanism at sliding-window boundaries to merge co-located tracks, preventing repeated observations of the same surface from redundantly accumulating. Second, we decompose tracking into an endpoint refiner that predicts each point's destination and static-versus-dynamic classification, followed by a lightweight trajectory refiner that decodes dense trajectories exclusively for dynamic points. Third, we propose 3D WAFT, replacing memory-prohibitive 4D correlation volumes with efficient feature sampling in the scene cloud. To the best of our knowledge, TrackEverything is the first 3D tracker capable of tracking all visible points across videos exceeding 1000 frames within 40 GB of GPU memory. On TAPVid-3D, TrackEverything outperforms all open-source all-frame dense 3D trackers by more than 20% APD on short clips, while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art sparse trackers on long sequences, despite tracking far more points.

</details>


### [4] [Accelerating Video Diffusion via Training-Free Trajectory Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30096)
*Mustafa Munir,Huy Vu,Shreyas Misra,Rohit Jena,Sajad Norouzi,Ali Taghibakhshi,Anis Ahmad,Anjul Patney,Pavlo Molchanov,Nima Tajbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACK 提出一种免训练、无需架构/调度器修改的异构去噪策略：通过校准阶段计算大/小模型预测的相对分歧分数图，确定切换策略，在质量敏感步保留大模型、低分歧步路由到小模型，从而降低平均单步成本。在 Wan 2.1、Cosmos 3、TurboDiffusion、FastVideo 上分别取得 1.95×、2.04×–2.73×、2.69×、2.17× 加速，质量相当且多样性保持良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型推理代价高昂：需在大量去噪步上执行大模型；即便采用步蒸馏，每一步仍需一次昂贵的大模型评估。因此需要一种能在去噪过程中降低平均单步计算成本、又不损失生成质量的加速方案。

Method: TRACK（TRajectory-Aware Capacity routing via top-K selection）采用异构去噪与模型切换：首先用大模型 rollout 一条参考轨迹；在每个去噪步额外采集小模型预测，并与大模型预测对比，得到相对分歧分数（两模型共享相同 latent、timestep、conditioning 与 guidance 输入）。在校准集上聚合该信号，形成跨扩散步的分歧分数图，据此确定切换策略（top-K 选择）：质量敏感步继续使用大模型，低分歧步路由至小模型。推理时每步仅执行所选模型，无需重训练、无需改动架构或调度器、也无需在线双模型评估。

Result: 在 Wan 2.1、Cosmos 3、TurboDiffusion、FastVideo 上，TRACK 分别实现 1.95×、2.04×–2.73×、2.69×、2.17× 的加速，同时保持可比的整体质量与较高的多样性保留率。

Conclusion: TRACK 确立了自动化、免训练模型切换作为视频扩散加速的一种实用范式。

Abstract: Video diffusion is computationally expensive, as it requires executing a large model across many denoising steps. Even with step-distillation, inference remains expensive because every distilled step still requires a costly model evaluation. We present TRACK: TRajectory-Aware Capacity routing via top-K selection, a heterogeneous denoising strategy that switches between compatible large and small models at selected steps, reducing the average cost per denoising evaluation. The switching steps are determined using a calibration process. TRACK first rolls out a reference trajectory with the large model. Then at each step, the small model's prediction is also collected and compared against the large model's prediction to obtain a relative disagreement score. Both models receive the same latent, timestep, conditioning, and guidance inputs. Aggregating this signal over a calibration set produces a disagreement score map across diffusion steps, which determines a switching policy for an efficient inference process: quality-sensitive steps keep using the large model, while steps with low disagreement scores are routed to the small model. Inference executes only the selected model at each step, requiring no retraining, architecture or scheduler changes, or online dual-model evaluation. Across Wan 2.1, Cosmos 3, TurboDiffusion, and FastVideo, TRACK yields $1.95\times$, $2.04\times$-$2.73\times$, $2.69\times$, and $2.17\times$ speedups, respectively, with comparable aggregate quality and high diversity retention. TRACK thereby establishes automated, training-free model switching as a practical acceleration paradigm for video diffusion.

</details>


### [5] [The Alignment Illusion in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30210)
*Hong-Han Wang,Yuntao Wang,Hu Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出 MLLM 中常见的视觉-文本标量对齐分数可能产生“对齐幻觉”：即使视觉输入被高斯噪声破坏、任务准确率大幅下降，CKA、SVCCA、MIR 和主角度余弦等指标仍无法稳定区分损坏流。作者将其归因于语言模型 MLP down-projection 造成的权重诱导、近一维对齐，并提出 PA gap 作为更能反映多方向视觉结构的几何诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常把 MLLM 中层间视觉-文本相似度解释为语言模型逐步将视觉内容整合进共享表示空间，但这一解读依赖一个未经验证的假设：标量对齐分数能够反映内容级跨模态交互。本文旨在检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 对视觉流进行受控干预：将 projector 输出的视觉 token 替换为高斯噪声，并在 13 个 MLLM、5 个模型家族、0.5B–72B 参数范围内测试 CKA、SVCCA、MIR 和 leading principal-angle cosine 等标量对齐指标。进一步提出 principal-angle gap（PA gap），定义为最高两个 principal-angle cosine 之差，以区分权重诱导的相似性与多方向视觉结构。

Result: 用高斯噪声替换视觉 token 会显著降低任务准确率，但四种标准标量指标无法一致地区分损坏视觉流与原始视觉流，作者称此为 alignment illusion。其根源在于共享语言模型路径：各向异性 MLP down-projection 将视觉与文本 token 拉向共同输出方向，形成权重诱导对齐，且该分量基本是一维的。PA gap 能分离这种权重诱导相似性与多方向视觉结构；在渐进视觉损坏下，PA gap 比标量分数更一致地跟踪任务准确率；在结构化但无关图像下，还能揭示内部几何与任务准确率分离的情形。

Conclusion: MLLM 内部的视觉-文本对齐不应被直接等同于内容级跨模态交互，而应被理解为语言模型内部视觉流的几何诊断；只有在受控任务证据校准下，这类内部几何指标才具有充分解释力。

Abstract: Layer-wise visual-text similarity in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) is widely interpreted as evidence that the language model progressively integrates visual content into a shared representation space. This reading rests on the assumption that scalar alignment scores reflect content-level cross-modal interaction. To test this assumption, we apply controlled interventions to the visual stream. Across 13 MLLMs from five families spanning 0.5B to 72B parameters, replacing projector-output visual tokens with Gaussian noise sharply reduces task accuracy, yet four standard scalar measures (CKA, SVCCA, MIR, and the leading principal-angle cosine) fail to consistently separate the corrupted stream from the original. We call this failure the alignment illusion and trace it to the shared language-model pathway: anisotropic MLP down-projections pull visual and text tokens toward common output directions, producing weight-induced alignment. Because this component is essentially one-dimensional, we introduce the principal-angle gap (PA gap), defined as the difference between the top two principal-angle cosines, which separates weight-induced similarity from multi-directional visual structure. Under graded visual corruption, the PA gap tracks task accuracy more consistently than the scalar scores we consider; under a structured but irrelevant image, it further exposes regimes in which internal geometry and task accuracy come apart. Internal visual-text alignment in MLLMs is therefore best read as a geometric diagnostic of the visual stream inside the language model rather than a direct proxy for content-level cross-modal interaction, and is most informative when calibrated by controlled task evidence.

</details>

### [6] [Underwater C3-JEPA: An Object-Centric Cross-View World Model for ROV Salvage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30214)
*Yuncong Yang,Jinlong Li,Yulong Xue,Feng Wu,Chunwen Zhang,Lei Qiao,Xuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向近场重载水下ROV打捞的物体中心多视角预测式世界模型C³-JEPA，在无接触传感器条件下，从多视角RGB与控制信号在潜空间预测任务对象状态演化，并用于MPC评估与想象式策略训练，真实水下视频验证了跨视角补全与优于持续基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近场重载水下ROV打捞中，接触传感难以部署，且车辆水动力滞后与接触交互使任务对象状态演化复杂；仅靠重建式或单视角表示难以提供可用于控制的预测接口。作者希望用无重建的潜空间预测世界模型，从多视角RGB和控制信号中学习任务相关状态表征，并支撑下游控制与策略学习。

Method: C³-JEPA（cross-view, control-conditioned, context-extended）将多相机观测编码为任务对象token与上下文token；通过留出视角注意力（held-out-view attention）融合跨相机证据；以控制为条件直接预测未来状态。采用弱绑定以低标注成本锚定目标与夹爪，并用SIGReg增强几何表征；预测器保持轻量。预测接口用于MPC候选评估与想象式rollout的行为智能体训练。

Result: 实验表明，学习到的表征相较无重建潜变量基线，能向下游探针传递显著更多任务相关信息，同时预测器保持轻量。真实水下视频验证显示，同一架构能够恢复被留出相机的物体状态，并持续优于persistence基线，说明该方案可跨出仿真迁移。

Conclusion: C³-JEPA提供了一种无接触传感、以多视角RGB与控制为输入的潜空间预测世界模型，可服务于MPC评估与想象式策略训练，并在真实水下场景中验证了跨视角状态恢复与预测有效性，具备从仿真向真实迁移的潜力。

Abstract: We present Underwater C$^{3}$-JEPA (cross-view, control-conditioned, context-extended), an object-centric multi-view predictive world model for near-field heavy-load underwater ROV salvage. Without contact sensors, it predicts in latent space how the task-object state evolves through contact interaction and under the hydrodynamic lag of the vehicle, from synchronized multi-view RGB observations and vehicle control signals. C$^{3}$-JEPA encodes multi-camera observations into task-object and context tokens, fuses cross-camera evidence through held-out-view attention, and directly predicts future states conditioned on control. Weak binding anchors the target and gripper at low annotation cost, while SIGReg sharpens the geometric representation. Experiments show that the learned representation transfers substantially more task-relevant information to downstream probes than a reconstruction-free latent baseline, while keeping the predictor lightweight. The resulting predictive interface supports model-predictive-control (MPC) candidate evaluation and imagined-rollout behavior-agent training. Validation on real underwater video shows the same architecture recovering a withheld camera's object state and staying ahead of persistence, so the recipe transfers beyond simulation.

</details>


### [7] [Coding Agents for Generalized Task and Motion Planning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30233)
*Matteo Merler,Bowen Li,Josh Roy,Yichao Liang,Qianwei Wang,Yixuan Huang,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究编码智能体能否自动合成可跨实例泛化的 TAMP 程序；在 28 个仿真环境、98,000 个评估回合中，三种智能体配置均优于手工规划器与基线，成功率 56%–95%，且随物体数量增长保持更高成功率、平均计算量低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义 TAMP 需利用问题实例间的规律降低新实例规划成本，但现有方法依赖大量 TAMP 专用工程。作者希望验证：在给定任务描述与模拟器访问权限、固定合成预算下，编码智能体能否通过与环境交互自动合成泛化程序。

Method: 将问题设定为程序合成：智能体自行决定如何与环境交互，在预算内开发程序，随后冻结程序并在未见实例上评估。在 KinDER 与 PDDLStream 的 28 个仿真环境中，使用超出原基准的物体数量；评估 Claude Code（Opus 5）与 Codex（GPT-5.6 Sol、GPT-6 Astra），并比较手工规划器、一次性生成及基于 LLM 的广义规划基线。

Result: 共评估 980 个生成程序，每个程序在 100 个留出实例上测试，总计 98,000 个评估回合。在 16 个有规划器可用的环境中，三种智能体配置平均成功率为 56%–95%，高于规划器的 47%；随物体数量增加，智能体程序成功率仍高于规划器，且平均每实例计算量约低一个数量级。日志显示智能体通过交互校准物理模型、测试边界情况并改进策略。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在广义 TAMP 中表现意外地有效，可作为强基线；作者发布全部代码及智能体提示词，表明该方向具有自动化程序合成替代大量专用工程的潜力。

Abstract: Task and motion planning (TAMP) problems remain difficult even with full observability and object-centric states because discrete decisions are tightly coupled to geometric, kinematic, and dynamic constraints. Generalized TAMP addresses this difficulty by exploiting regularities across problem instances to reduce planning effort on new instances. However, existing methods require substantial TAMP-specific engineering. We investigate whether coding agents can automate this process by synthesizing programs that generalize across instances. Given a task description and simulator access, each agent chooses how to interact with the environment while developing a program within a fixed synthesis budget. The program is then frozen and evaluated on unseen instances. We evaluate Claude Code (Opus 5) and Codex (GPT-5.6 Sol and GPT-6 Astra) on 28 simulated environments from KinDER and PDDLStream, with object counts beyond those evaluated in the original benchmark. Across all program synthesis methods, we evaluate 980 generated programs on 100 held-out instances each, 98,000 evaluation episodes in total. Overall, we find that coding agents are surprisingly effective at generalized TAMP: all three agent configurations outperform hand-engineered planners, one-shot generation, and an LLM-based generalized planning baseline in mean success (56% to 95% versus 47% for the planners, on the 16 environments where a planner is available). As object counts grow, the agents' programs maintain higher success than the planner, using an order of magnitude less computation per instance on average. Logs show agents using interaction to calibrate physical models, test edge cases, and refine strategies. We release all code, including the full prompts given to the agents. These findings suggest that coding agents are a strong baseline for generalized TAMP.

</details>


### [8] [Rolling-WAM: World Action Models with Rolling Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30247)
*Yinghua Zhou,Junjie Ye,Yiqi Zhao,Hao Dong,Celina Shiyu Wang,Ruohai Ge,Tingyi Yang,Basile Van Hoorick,Gaurav Sukhatme,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rolling-WAM 将视频-动作联合去噪过程分散到连续的重规划周期中，通过滑动窗口与滚动噪声调度，在保持操作性能的同时实现约 4.5 倍的稳态重规划加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Models（WAMs）在每个重规划周期都要完整执行视频-动作联合去噪，导致显著延迟，动作更新滞后，闭环响应能力受限。

Method: 提出 Rolling-WAM：维护一个处于交错噪声水平的视频-动作块滑动窗口，并采用滚动噪声调度。每一步对即将执行的近端动作块完全去噪，对更远未来的块仅部分精炼；随着新相机观测到来窗口前移，保留的未来块继续去噪，从而将计算成本分摊到时间上，并在块边界间携带演化的视觉-动作上下文。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 以及真实 Unitree G1 人形机器人上评估，Rolling-WAM 取得了有竞争力的操作性能；相比标准联合 WAM，无需从头对整个预测时域去噪，实现 4.5 倍稳态重规划加速。

Conclusion: Rolling-WAM 通过跨重规划周期的分布式联合去噪，有效降低闭环控制延迟并提升响应性，同时保持较强操作性能，为实时机器人操作中的世界动作模型提供可行方案。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple action generation with future visual prediction for robotic manipulation. However, completing the joint video-action denoising process at each replanning cycle incurs substantial latency, delaying action updates and limiting closed-loop responsiveness. We present Rolling-WAM, a formulation that distributes joint denoising across successive replanning cycles. Our method maintains a sliding window of video-action chunks at staggered noise levels. At each step, a rolling noise schedule fully denoises the imminent action chunk for execution, while partially refining farther-future chunks. As the window advances with new camera observations, the retained future chunks continue their denoising process. This distributes the computational cost over time while carrying an evolving visual-action context across chunk boundaries. Evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and a real-world Unitree G1 humanoid show that Rolling-WAM achieves competitive manipulation performance. By removing the need to denoise the entire prediction horizon from scratch, it delivers a 4.5x steady-state replanning speedup over standard joint WAMs.

</details>


### [9] [RAPID: Robot Agentic Programming from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30249)
*Yuyao Liu,Jiayuan Mao,David Hsu,Leslie Pack Kaelbling,Tomás Lozano-Pérez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPID 提出一种"机器人智能体式编程"框架：仅凭单次人类视觉演示，即可自动生成、验证并迭代精化机器人程序，实现跨物体位姿、形状、材质与环境的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体（coding agents）在复杂编程任务上已取得巨大成功，但如何将其能力迁移到机器人系统尚缺乏有效途径。传统从演示学习（LfD）往往难以生成可验证、可复用、可跨场景迁移的程序，因此需要一种能将演示自动转化为可测试、可执行、可迭代改进的机器人程序的方法。

Method: 给定单次视觉人类演示，RAPID 自动推断代码精化闭环所需的三项要素：(i) 可测试的任务规范；(ii) 用于机器人执行的动作原语；(iii) 支持程序执行与验证的交互式环境。为提升程序在演示场景之外的可复用性，采用"物体中心的关系式程序表示"：将动作原语表达为轨迹优化程序以实现物体级运动效果，并通过关系约束在运行时组合，从而捕获场景特定几何关系，关注演示策略的底层结构而非具体运动轨迹。

Result: 在仿真中对八项具挑战性的接触丰富非抓取（nonprehensile）操作任务以及 LIBERO-Pro 基准中的通用抓取（prehensile）操作任务进行评估；同时在真实 Franka 机械臂上完成部署，并对全部八项非抓取任务进行评测。所有实验中 RAPID 均表现强劲，并在物体位姿、形状、材质和环境方面展现出良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: RAPID 验证了借助编码智能体的"自动生成—验证—精化"循环，可从单次演示中构建可复用、可泛化的机器人程序；配合物体中心的关系式表示与轨迹优化原语，方法在仿真与真机上均取得有效结果，为机器人程序的自动编程提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Coding agents have demonstrated enormous success in solving complex programming problems. To leverage their potential for robot systems, this work introduces Robot Agentic Programming from Demonstrations (RAPID), which automatically generates, verifies, and refines robot programs, given a single visual human demonstration. The iterative agentic loop of code refinement requires several key ingredients: (i) a testable task specification, (ii) action primitives for robot execution, and (iii) an interactive environment for program execution and verification. RAPID infers all three from the demonstration automatically. To make the resulting program reusable beyond the demonstration setting, RAPID uses an object-centric relational program representation that focuses on the underlying structure of the demonstrated strategy rather than the specific motion per se: it expresses the action primitives as trajectory-optimization programs that realize object-level motion effects, while composing them through relational constraints that capture scene-specific geometry at run time. We evaluated RAPID in simulation on eight challenging contact-rich nonprehensile manipulation tasks as well as general prehensile manipulation tasks in the LIBERO-Pro benchmark. We also successfully deployed it on a real Franka arm and evaluated on all eight nonprehensile tasks. In all experiments, RAPID demonstrated strong performance, with generalization over object pose, shape, material, and environment. Website: https://yuyaoliu.me/projects/rapid.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [10] [Thinking Leakage: A Causal Audit of NoThink Post-Training in Hybrid Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28682)
*Zehao Liu,Vasant G. Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用因果中介与激活方向双向干预审计 NoThink 模式后训练增益，发现其相当一部分来自对基座模型已有 Think 行为的“思维泄漏”，泄漏比例达 42%–79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NoThink 模式下后训练混合推理模型虽能提升性能并保持推理速度，但这些增益可能来自基座模型 Think 模式中已可访问的思考行为，而非 NoThink 能力本身；需要因果性地审计这种“思维泄漏”。

Method: 将“思维泄漏”形式化为因果中介框架，并沿一条由基座模型导出的简单激活方向进行双向干预：对基座模型沿该方向引导，对后训练检查点进行反向引导；在 3 个模型、3 种后训练方法和竞赛数学基准上开展行为与表征分析。

Result: 泄漏真实、因果且显著：行为与表征分析显示模型向 Think 偏移；沿该方向引导基座模型可复现大部分后训练准确率增益；对检查点反向引导可消除其相当一部分增益。在 9 个具有正向 NoThink 增益的对齐检查点上，泄漏比例为 42%–79%。

Conclusion: 后训练方法在 NoThink 模式下的表面优势可能主要反映模型向 Think 行为的漂移，而非在 NoThink 内部真正提升能力；这提示评估混合推理模型时需区分“能力提升”与“已有 Think 行为的重新调用”。

Abstract: Post-training hybrid reasoning models in NoThink mode has attracted growing interest as a way to improve performance while keeping inference fast. However, these gains may draw on thinking behavior already accessible through the base model's Think mode. We formulate this thinking leakage in a causal mediation framework and audit its contribution using bidirectional interventions along a simple base-derived activation direction. Across three models and three post-training methods on competition math benchmarks, we find that leakage is real, causal, and substantial: behavioral and representational analyses reveal shifts toward Think, steering the base model along this direction reproduces most of the post-training accuracy gain, and counter-steering a checkpoint removes a substantial share of what it gains. Across nine aligned checkpoints with positive NoThink gains, the resulting leakage ratio ranges from 42% to 79%. These interventions support a substantial causal contribution of thinking leakage. Our findings show that a post-training method's apparent advantage can therefore reflect greater drift toward Think, obscuring whether it improves capability within NoThink or more effectively re-invokes existing Think behavior.

</details>


### [11] [CataOPD: Catalytic On-Policy Distillation for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29518)
*Wenjin Liu,Chenxi Wang,Jiapu Wang,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CataOPD：将教师作为“催化剂”而非模仿目标，通过自救援路由、催化引导自解决和障碍加权内化，把经核验的学生生成轨迹内化进无催化剂策略，以提升 LLM 推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）在未采样到正确轨迹时缺乏正向正确性信号；同策略蒸馏（OPD）则受限于学生同策略分布下可达的推理轨迹。二者均存在可达性与正确信号不足的问题。

Method: CataOPD 包含三项机制：1）Self-Rescue Routing：将经验上全部失败的组作为路由信号而非教师干预触发器，先通过额外同策略自采样寻找正确轨迹；2）Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution：对自救援后仍未解决的问题，使用催化引导在引导学生分布中激发经核验的学生生成轨迹；3）Barrier-Weighted Internalization：按引导与未引导对数概率差对 token 加权，聚焦无引导时困难的决定性 token。

Result: 实验表明 CataOPD 优于现有基线，可将学生独立推理扩展到仍未恢复的问题，并在无催化剂推理下提升分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 教师可作为过程性催化剂扩展学生可达轨迹，同时将经核验的学生生成轨迹内化到无催化剂策略中，兼顾可达性、正确信号与独立推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD) are two representative paradigms for improving large language model reasoning. However, when no correct trajectory is sampled, RL lacks a positive correctness signal, while OPD remains constrained by the reasoning trajectories reachable under the student's on-policy distribution. Therefore, we propose CataOPD, where the teacher acts as a catalyst rather than a target, expanding reachability while internalizing verified student-produced trajectories into a catalyst-free policy. Self-Rescue Routing uses empirically all-failed groups as routing signals rather than teacher-intervention triggers, first seeking correct trajectories through additional on-policy self-sampling. For problems unresolved after self-rescue, Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution uses catalytic guidance to elicit a verified student-produced trajectory in the guided student distribution. Barrier-Weighted Internalization weights tokens by guided-to-unguided log-probability gaps, focusing updates on decisive tokens difficult without guidance. Experimental results show that CataOPD outperforms current baselines, extends independent student reasoning to still-unrecovered problems, and improves out-of-distribution generalization under catalyst-free inference. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/CataOPD.

</details>


### [12] [Beyond Average Safety: Chance-Constrained LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29960)
*Taha Entesari,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种机会约束式安全保持微调方法，通过可微的违规率majorization与约束感知梯度下降，限制安全样本退化比例，在多个任务和模型上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调虽能提升有用性、指令遵循或领域性能，但可能损害安全关键提示的表现。现有方法多控制平均安全损失或使用加权辅助惩罚，容易掩盖罕见但严重的失败案例。

Method: 将安全保持微调建模为机会约束优化：限制相对于参考模型退化超过给定阈值的安全样本比例。由于经验机会约束包含不连续指示函数，作者引入违规率的可微majorization，得到可处理的保守约束；进而提出约束感知梯度下降，将majorized约束视为参数空间中的安全集，以闭式解最小修正微调方向，形成尾部感知的安全校正，重点处理接近或超过退化阈值的样本。

Result: 在三个任务、三个模型上进行了大量有害微调实验，所提方法持续优于文献中的基线方法。

Conclusion: LLM微调中的安全保持更适合被看作可靠性约束优化问题，而非平均风险正则化问题。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on new objectives can improve helpfulness, instruction following, or domain-specific performance, but it can also induce regressions on safety-critical prompts. Existing safety-preserving fine-tuning methods typically control average safety loss or use weighted auxiliary penalties, which can obscure rare but severe failures. We propose a chance-constrained formulation for safety-preserving fine-tuning that limits the fraction of safety examples whose degradation relative to a reference model exceeds a prescribed threshold. Because the resulting empirical chance constraint contains a discontinuous indicator, we introduce a differentiable majorization of the violation rate, yielding a tractable conservative constraint. We then develop a constraint-aware gradient descent method that treats the majorized constraint as a safe set in parameter space and minimally modifies the fine-tuning direction to preserve feasibility. The resulting update admits a closed form and produces a tail-aware safety correction that emphasizes examples near or above the degradation threshold. We conduct an extensive set of experiments on harmful fine-tuning across three different tasks and three models and show that our approach consistently outperforms the baselines that exist in the literature. These results suggest that safety preservation in LLM fine-tuning is better viewed as a reliability-constrained optimization problem than as average-risk regularization.

</details>


### [13] [Diverse Geometries, Frozen Weights: Robust Heterogeneous Treatment-Effect Estimation via Causal Expert Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29974)
*Ali Haghpanah Jahromi,Mohammad Taheri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoACE 是一个由五个专家组成的异构处理效应估计框架：以"锚点-校正"估计器为核心，结合重叠感知与结果引导的多种几何结构；集成权重仅依据内部验证预测学习、冻结后再应用于全样本重拟合的专家。实验表明，加入无结果信息的重叠感知专家 O-Phi-ACE 可在多数基准上降低 sqrt(PEHE)，但整体统计显著性不足，说明其价值在于几何多样性与无泄漏聚合的稳健性，而非普遍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据估计异质处理效应（HTE）困难，因为最优的归纳偏置（inductive bias）会随重叠度、处理组不平衡、预后结构（prognostic structure）与样本量而变化，单一模型难以在所有情形下保持优势。因此需要一种能够自适应整合多种归纳偏置、同时避免测试信息泄漏的稳健估计方案。

Method: 提出 GeoACE（Geometry-Diverse Anchor-Correction Expert Ensemble），一个五专家框架：以共同的"锚点-校正"（anchor-correction）估计器为基础，搭配互补的"重叠感知"与"结果引导"几何结构。集成权重在任务层面仅由内部验证预测学习，在测试评估前冻结，再应用于在完整开发样本上重拟合的专家。第五个专家 O-Phi-ACE 由协变量与处理分配构造一个无结果、重叠感知的统计投影（低维几何），并用该几何替换锚点输入。

Result: 在八个基准协议、11 个对比方法上评估：加入 O-Phi-ACE 后，在全部七个具有个体效应真值的基准上相对四专家集成降低了平均 sqrt(PEHE)，在 1,225 个配对任务中胜出 998 个；JOBS 策略风险变化可忽略。五专家集成在 IHDP100、IHDPA、IHDPB 上排名第一，在 NEWS 上排名第二（与榜首相差 0.13%）。在七个 sqrt(PEHE) 基准上取得最低平均排名 3.714，但 Friedman 与 Iman-Davenport 检验不显著（p=0.328、0.330）。使用同一组五个冻结专家时，inverse-DR 加权在基准均衡分析中持续优于 winner-take-all、convex DR 拟合、R-stacking 与 causal Q-aggregation，但与等权及 DR ridge 收缩在统计上无法区分。

Conclusion: 证据支持"几何多样化专家库 + 无泄漏聚合"作为一种稳健性策略，而非 GeoACE 本身或某一加权规则的普遍优越性；即在平均表现上有改善，但尚缺乏统计显著的整体优势。

Abstract: Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects from observational data is difficult because the most appropriate inductive bias varies with overlap, treatment imbalance, prognostic structure, and sample size. We introduce the Geometry-Diverse Anchor-Correction Expert Ensemble (GeoACE), a five-expert framework that combines a common anchor-correction estimator with complementary overlap-aware and outcome-guided geometries. Its task-level ensemble weights are learned only from internal validation predictions, frozen before test evaluation, and then applied to experts refitted on the complete development sample. The fifth expert, O-Phi-ACE, constructs an outcome-free, overlap-aware statistical projection from covariates and treatment assignment and replaces the anchor input with this lower-dimensional geometry. We evaluate GeoACE against 11 comparators on eight benchmark protocols. Adding O-Phi-ACE reduced mean sqrt(PEHE) relative to the four-expert ensemble on all seven benchmarks with individual-effect truth, winning 998 of 1,225 paired tasks; the change on JOBS policy risk was negligible. The five-expert ensemble ranked first on IHDP100, IHDPA, and IHDPB and second on NEWS, differing from the NEWS leader by 0.13%. Across the seven sqrt(PEHE) benchmarks it obtained the lowest observed average rank (3.714), although the omnibus Friedman and Iman-Davenport tests were not significant (p=0.328 and p=0.330). Using the same five frozen experts, inverse-DR weighting was consistently better than winner-take-all selection, convex DR fitting, R-stacking, and causal Q-aggregation in benchmark-balanced analyses, but was statistically indistinguishable from equal weighting and DR ridge shrinkage. The evidence therefore supports geometry-diverse expert libraries and leakage-free aggregation as a robustness strategy, not universal superiority of either GeoACE or one weighting rule.

</details>


### [14] [Let Training Guide Selection: Online Synthetic Data Filtering via Real-Anchored Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29988)
*Yanran Wu,Sana Lakdawala,Renzo Tassara Miller,Chongyang Bai,Sharath Ciddu,Shivendra Pratap Singh,Kungang Li,Sandeep Pandey,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FROST 通过真实数据锚定的梯度反馈在线评估合成数据效用，仅在异常批次中过滤约 20%–30% 样本，无需外部验证器，在图像分类、文本到 SQL 微调及工业广告重排序中均优于全量合成数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可在真实数据有限时扩展监督信号，但噪声与分布不匹配会降低其价值。现有合成数据选择方法多强调保真度或多样性，而非学习者不断变化的需求。

Method: 提出 FROST 在线框架：以真实训练数据为锚点，通过梯度反馈估计合成数据的效用；将批次效用与近期历史进行校准，以判断何时需要过滤；仅对“超出正常波动范围”（out-of-band）的批次进行样本筛选，以决定保留哪些样本。整个流程不依赖外部验证器或留出验证集。

Result: 在图像分类与面向文本到 SQL 的 LLM 微调两个公开基准上，FROST 过滤掉约 20%–30% 的合成数据，同时相比使用完整合成数据池训练提升了真实任务性能。进一步将 FROST 应用于大规模工业广告重排序系统的训练中，在高度优化的生产基线上取得显著性能增益，表明其有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: FROST 是一种无需外部验证器或留出验证集的在线合成数据筛选框架，能够依据学习者的实时需求动态选择合成样本，并在公开基准与大规模工业系统中验证了其有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Synthetic data can scale training supervision when real-world data are limited, but noise and distribution mismatch can reduce its value. Existing synthetic data selection methods often emphasize fidelity or diversity rather than the learner's evolving needs. We propose FROST, an online framework that estimates synthetic-data utility through gradient feedback anchored in real training data. It calibrates batch utility against recent history to determine when filtering is needed and filters samples only in out-of-band batches to determine what to retain, without an external verifier or held-out validation set. Experiments on two public benchmarks for image classification and LLM fine-tuning for text-to-SQL show that FROST filters out around 20--30% of the synthetic data while improving real-task performance compared with training on the full synthetic data pool. We further apply FROST during training in a large-scale industrial ads re-ranking system, achieving significant performance gains over a highly optimized production baseline, demonstrating its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [15] [Reachability-Based Formal Verification of Graph Neural Networks with Node and Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30079)
*Anne M. Tumlin,Ben Wooding,Zhenxuan Shao,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Tyler Derr,Taylor T. Johnson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GNNV，用GraphStar集将NNV扩展到图结构输入，支持GCN/GINE消息传递与ReLU的可靠验证，在电力系统PF/OPF/CFA及图分类任务上给出更紧的鲁棒性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN已成为电力系统快速、拓扑感知代理模型的重要方法，广泛用于潮流分析、最优潮流估计和连锁故障分析；但GNN模型的形式化验证仍具挑战，现有方法验证范围有限，尤其缺乏对节点与边特征联合不确定性的处理。

Method: 将神经网络验证（NNV）框架扩展至图结构输入，提出GraphStar集——Star集的推广，可刻画节点和边特征上的不确定性；据此推导线性消息传递操作的传播规则，并对ReLU非线性进行可靠过近似，覆盖GCN与GINE等GNN架构。

Result: 在IEEE-24、IEEE-39、IEEE-118测试系统上的PF、OPF、CFA三项电力系统任务，以及ENZYMES、PROTEINS两个图分类基准上评估GNNV。结果表明：对含ReLU激活的图分类模型，GNNV比CORA提供更紧的鲁棒性保证；并首次为基于GINE的PF与OPF模型在节点和边联合扰动下提供边感知鲁棒性保证。

Conclusion: GNNV可作为图结构GNN的通用验证框架，尤其适用于电力系统代理模型的鲁棒性认证，并为边特征不确定性下的验证提供了新能力。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have become a prominent approach for developing fast, topology-aware surrogates in electric power systems, supporting tasks such as power flow (PF) analysis, optimal power flow (OPF) estimation, and cascading failure analysis (CFA). Despite this growing use, formally verifying GNN-based models remains challenging, with existing methods limited in scope. We extend the neural network verification (NNV) framework to graph-structured inputs through GraphStar sets, a generalization of Star sets that captures uncertainty over both node and edge features. This extension enables the propagation of linear message-passing operations and the sound approximation of ReLU nonlinearities for GNN architectures, including graph convolutional network (GCN) and graph isomorphism network with edge features (GINE) layers. We evaluate GNNV across three power system tasks, PF, OPF, and CFA, on the IEEE-24, IEEE-39, and IEEE-118 test cases, as well as two standard graph classification benchmarks, ENZYMES and PROTEINS. Our results show that GNNV provides tighter robustness guarantees than CORA on graph classification models with ReLU-based activations and, for the first time, delivers edge-aware robustness guarantees for GINE-based PF and OPF models under joint node and edge perturbations.

</details>


### [16] [Residual Correlation as a Diagnostic for Joint-Uncertainty Gains from GP Coregionalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30085)
*Fangqin Zhou,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出多目标回归中GP-ICM的核心价值在于联合不确定性量化而非点预测，并提出轻量级诊断量D_logdet与Residual-ICM方法，用于判断残差协方差耦合何时有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标回归中，相关目标常通过带内在核心区域化模型的多输出高斯过程（GP-ICM）耦合，假设共享统计强度能提升整体性能。然而实践中这种收益并不稳定，作者希望厘清核心区域化究竟在何种情形下真正带来好处，并找出能预测其收益的指标。

Method: 作者首先区分原始目标相关性与残差相关性（即独立逐目标预测器无法解释的跨目标依赖），发现后者才是联合不确定性收益的最强预测因子。据此提出轻量级诊断量 D_logdet = -1/2·log det R_res，表示用完整残差协方差替代对角协方差时的理想化联合负对数似然（NLL）增益，可仅由独立GP计算得到。进一步提出 Residual-ICM，在保持独立边缘方差的同时向联合协方差中引入残差相关结构。

Result: 在受控合成实验、16个多目标基准，以及用于关键点回归的冻结Transformer与卷积神经网络表征上，点预测基本不变（ΔR²≈0）。相比之下，D_logdet能强预测观测到的ICM NLL改进（ρ_s=-0.83，p<0.001），优于特征-样本比等启发式指标。Residual-ICM在所比较方法中取得最佳平均联合NLL。

Conclusion: 核心区域化的主要收益来自联合不确定性量化而非点预测，且原始目标相关性无法预测耦合是否有益，残差相关性才是关键。D_logdet可指示协方差耦合何时有用，但该诊断仅适用于可分离核心区域化所刻画的全局高斯残差依赖结构。

Abstract: In multi-target regression, correlated targets are often coupled through multi-output Gaussian processes with an intrinsic model of coregionalisation (GP-ICM), assuming that sharing statistical strength improves overall performance. In practice, the benefits are inconsistent. Across the settings studied, we find that the main benefit of coregionalisation is joint uncertainty quantification rather than point prediction. Raw target correlation does not predict when coupling helps; in the separable GP-ICM settings studied here, residual correlation, the cross-target dependence left unexplained by independent per-target predictors, is the strongest predictor of joint-uncertainty gains.
  We introduce a lightweight diagnostic, $D_{\rm logdet}=-\frac{1}{2}\log\det R_{\rm res}$, which represents the idealised joint negative log-likelihood (NLL) gain from modelling a full rather than diagonal residual covariance and is computable from independent GPs alone. Across a controlled synthetic study, 16 multi-target benchmarks, and frozen transformer and convolutional neural network representations for keypoint regression, point prediction remains largely unchanged ($ΔR^2\approx 0$). In contrast, $D_{\rm logdet}$ strongly predicts observed ICM NLL improvements ($ρ_s=-0.83$, $p<0.001$), outperforming heuristics such as the feature-to-sample ratio. We also propose Residual-ICM, which preserves independent marginal variances while adding residual-correlation structure to the joint covariance. Residual-ICM achieves the best average joint NLL among the compared methods, while the diagnostic indicates when covariance coupling is likely to be useful. The diagnostic is specific to global Gaussian residual dependence, the structure captured by separable coregionalisation.

</details>


### [17] [AT-SKM-Net: An Accelerated Trainable Sampling Kaczmarz-Motzkin Framework for Linear Hard-Constraint Feasibility on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30088)
*Xiaochen Zhang,Haoyu Zhu,Yao Zhang,Qingchun Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AT-SKM Net框架，用拓扑感知异质GNN引导混合采样并结合Cholesky更新，加速图结构线性约束优化中的可训练SKM投影，同时保持零约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构线性约束优化是关键基础设施中的基础问题，但大规模严格硬约束与高维性带来严重可扩展性瓶颈。现有T-SKM-Net等投影方法虽能保证可行性，却需处理全部约束并进行昂贵矩阵分解，在动态环境下计算成本过高。

Method: 提出加速可训练SKM网络（AT-SKM）。核心包括：1）设计由拓扑感知异质GNN引导的混合采样策略，将计算集中于活跃约束并消除冗余计算；2）引入Cholesky更新机制以高效应对图约束的拓扑变化，理论上在低秩扰动下将等式投影复杂度从O(N^3)降至O(N^2)。

Result: 在随机几何图、N-1安全约束DC-OPF以及最小成本天然气运输问题上验证：AT-SKM将迭代次数最多减少85%，SKM层获得2.95倍至7.29倍加速，同时保持零约束违反。

Conclusion: AT-SKM通过活跃约束聚焦采样与低秩Cholesky更新，有效缓解了动态图结构线性约束优化中的计算瓶颈，在不牺牲可行性的前提下显著提升效率，为大规模基础设施优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Graph-structured optimization with linear constraints is fundamental to critical infrastructure but faces scalability limits due to massive strict hard constraints and high dimensionality. While recent projection-based methods such as Trainable Sampling Kaczmarz-Motzkin Net (T-SKM-Net) guarantee feasibility, they face high computational costs in dynamic environments by processing the entire constraint set and requiring expensive matrix factorizations. To bridge this gap, we propose the Accelerated Trainable-SKM (AT-SKM) Net framework. To concentrate computation on the active constraints and eliminate redundant calculations, we introduce a hybrid sampling strategy guided by a topology-aware heterogeneous GNN model. To efficiently handle topological shifts in graph-based constraints, we employ a Cholesky Update mechanism that theoretically reduces the equality projection complexity from O(N^3) to O(N^2) under low-rank perturbations. Experiments on random geometric graphs, N-1 Security-Constrained DC-OPF, and minimum-cost gas transport problem demonstrate that AT-SKM reduces iteration counts by up to 85% and achieves 2.95x-7.29x SKM layer speedups, while maintaining zero constraint violations.

</details>


### [18] [On the SoS Certifiability of Log-Concave Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30105)
*Aleksandr Storozhenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文证明：对任意各向同性对数凹分布 \(P\)，多项式 \((Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m-\mathbb E_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m\) 对任意偶数 \(m\ge2\) 都是平方和；由此移除 Kothari–Steinhardt 结果中对 Poincaré 常数的依赖，恢复对数凹分布的最优矩界，并给出高维统计估计中具有维度无关误差保证的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于对数凹分布矩界/平方和证书的结果依赖 Poincaré 常数，未能达到最优矩界，也限制了高维统计估计中维度无关误差保证。本文旨在消除该依赖，建立更优的矩证书并推广算法应用。

Method: 核心工具是随机局部化：将 \(P\) 分解为随机强对数凹测度的平均；这些强对数凹测度的中心矩具有 Diakonikolas–Hopkins–Pensia–Tiegel（STOC 2025）给出的次高斯证书。作者采用协方差自适应的局部化选择，并证明由 Letwin 关于二次型的方差不等式导出的四阶矩证书足以在每个偶数阶控制该平均过程。

Result: 证明了主定理：存在通用常数 \(C>0\)，使对任意偶数 \(m\ge2\)，\((Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m-\mathbb E_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m\) 为平方和。该结果移除了 Kothari–Steinhardt 定理中的 Poincaré 常数依赖，恢复了对数凹分布的最优矩界。作为直接推论，得到一类高维统计估计问题的计算高效算法，并具有维度无关误差保证。

Conclusion: 本文通过对数凹分布的随机局部化分解与协方差自适应四阶矩证书，建立了任意偶数阶的最优平方和矩不等式，从而统一改进了对数凹分布矩界及其高维统计估计应用。

Abstract: For an arbitrary isotropic log-concave distribution $P$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$, we prove that the polynomial $(Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m - \mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m$ is a sum of squares for every even $m\ge2$, where $C>0$ is a universal constant. This removes the dependence on the Poincaré constant in the theorem of Kothari and Steinhardt (arXiv:1711.07465), recovering the optimal moment bounds for log-concave distributions. As an immediate corollary, we obtain computationally efficient algorithms with dimension-free error guarantees for a wide range of high-dimensional statistical estimation problems.
  Our proof uses stochastic localization to decompose $P$ as an average of random strongly log-concave measures, whose centered moments admit the subgaussian certificates of Diakonikolas, Hopkins, Pensia, and Tiegel (STOC 2025; arXiv:2410.21194). With a covariance-adapted choice of localization, we show that a fourth-moment certificate derived from Letwin's variance inequality for quadratic forms (arXiv:2607.24164) suffices to control this averaging at every even degree.

</details>


### [19] [Intrinsic-Extrinsic Coupling in Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30185)
*Qinyou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将“内在—外在耦合”操作化：用受约束学习状态干预的延续条件价值来刻画“当前观察不必决定后续训练响应”，并通过可执行的有限帧分类器头写入、匹配四格交互识别与闭环协调实验，将可执行状态几何与延续条件价值联系起来，同时把已识别耦合与完整策略性能区分开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习者当前观察未必决定其对后续训练的响应；需要形式化内在干预与外部延续之间的耦合，并避免把局部可容许、延续条件干预价值和完整策略性能混为一谈。

Method: 提出以延续为条件的受约束学习状态干预价值，用观察相对纤维描述当前一致性；设计可执行的有限帧分类器头写入，在保护当前logits的同时修复指定历史间隔，并施加有限精度接受检验；通过匹配四格对比识别同一内在干预与不同外部延续之间的读出特定非可加性；结合数学分析区分可行局部修复、有利终端输出与训练可达修复区域，并开展协调测试。

Result: 在基于CLINC的类增量设定中，回放使该写入的32次更新贡献从5个正确预测变为0；输出蒸馏、RoBERTa骨干与优化器原生SGDW动态下也出现非零交互。SGDW下，128次更新时三个激活根的 correct-count 交互均为负，表明耦合不必然带来正向协同。内容控制可匹配或超过开发增益；含Fiber的五根新测试比较显示 correct-count 效应依根而异，而非普遍有益。在次要交叉熵读出上，引导分配在全部五对中平均损失低于标准回放。

Conclusion: 这些结果通过连接可执行状态几何、延续条件价值、匹配交互识别与闭环协调，使内在—外在耦合具备可操作性，并将已识别的耦合与完整策略性能明确分离。

Abstract: A learner's current observations need not determine its response to further training. We formulate intrinsic-extrinsic coupling through the continuation-conditioned value of a constrained learning-state intervention, with observation-relative fibers describing present agreement. An executable finite-frame classifier-head write protects current logits while repairing specified historical margins under finite-precision acceptance checks. We distinguish local admissibility, continuation-conditioned intervention value, and complete-policy performance. A matched four-cell contrast identifies readout-specific non-additivity between the same intrinsic intervention and alternative external continuations. In a CLINC-derived class-incremental setting, replay changes the write's 32-update contribution from five correct predictions to zero. Nonzero interactions also occur under output distillation, with a RoBERTa backbone, and under optimizer-native SGDW dynamics. Under SGDW, correct-count interactions are negative in all three activated roots at 128 updates, showing that coupling need not imply positive synergy. The mathematical analysis distinguishes feasible local repairs and favorable terminal outputs from training-reachable repair regions. Separate coordination tests show that content controls match or exceed the development gain, while a five-root fresh-test comparison with Fiber present in every arm shows root-dependent rather than uniformly beneficial correct-count effects. On the secondary cross-entropy readout, guided allocation yields lower mean loss than standard replay in all five pairs. Together, these results make intrinsic-extrinsic coupling operational by connecting executable state geometry to continuation-conditioned value, matched interaction identification, and closed-loop coordination, while separating identified coupling from complete-policy performance.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Compression: Training Latent Representations for Stable Long-Horizon Rollout in Neural Surrogate Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30198)
*Andreas E. Robertson,Ashley T. Lenau,John D. Shimanek,Benjamin A. Jasperson,Vivek Oommen,David L. Damm,Krishna Garikipati,Remi Dingreville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对潜空间神经常微分/动力学代理模型在长自回归推演中误差快速累积的问题，作者指出不稳定并非源自潜表示本身，而是仅用重构目标训练出的潜空间不适配长时预测。通过 Koopman 算子学习、Hamming 噪声注入、噪声注入与多步 rollout 微调等训练干预，长推演误差降低约 40%，在两个物理基准上匹配或超越全分辨率模型，同时减少约两个数量级 FLOPs 和一半 GPU 内存，并在介观晶体塑性高周疲劳模拟中实现远超训练时域的外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间神经代理求解器理论上可通过在压缩潜空间演化来降低计算成本并简化学习，但实际长自回归 rollout 中误差迅速累积，限制预测效用。作者希望厘清不稳定的来源，并说明问题不在于潜表示本身，而在于其训练目标与长时预测不匹配。

Method: 系统评估训练层面的干预：在自编码器训练中引入 Koopman 算子学习和 Hamming 噪声注入以改善压缩；在动力学学习中引入噪声注入和多步 rollout 微调以改善长期演化。比较重构、单步预测和长 rollout 误差，并与全分辨率模型在计算量和显存上对比；进一步应用于介观晶体塑性高周疲劳模拟。

Result: 改善长时 rollout 稳定性的干预常会降低重构和单步预测等常规指标；综合干预使长 rollout 误差降低约 40%，在两个物理基准上匹配或超越全分辨率模型，FLOPs 少两个数量级，GPU 内存减半；在晶体塑性高周疲劳模拟中，代理模型可稳定外推至训练时域数个数量级之外。

Conclusion: 神经压缩不应仅以降维为目标，而应重构解空间以支持稳定的动力学演化；这是构建可靠、高效科学神经代理模型的关键要求。

Abstract: Latent neural surrogate solvers, or latent dynamics models, accelerate simulations of time-dependent physical systems by evolving a compressed latent space rather than resolving full-resolution fields directly. In principle this reduces computational cost and simplifies learning, but in practice errors often accumulate rapidly during long autoregressive rollouts, limiting predictive utility. We show that this instability does not stem from the latent representation itself, but arises when it is trained solely for reconstruction, producing representations poorly suited to long-horizon forecasting. We systematically evaluate training-level interventions that align latent representations with long-horizon rollout: Koopman operator learning and Hamming noise injection during autoencoder training to improve compression, together with noise injection and multi-step rollout fine-tuning to improve dynamics. Interventions that improve long-horizon rollout stability often degrade conventional training metrics, including reconstruction and one-step prediction accuracy. Collectively, these interventions reduce long-rollout error by approximately 40\% and match or exceed the accuracy of full-resolution models on two physics benchmarks, while requiring 2 orders of magnitude fewer floating point operations and half the GPU memory. Applied to mesoscale crystal-plasticity simulations of high-cycle fatigue, the resulting surrogate achieves stable extrapolation over horizons orders of magnitude beyond those observed during training. More broadly, these results show that neural compression should be designed not merely to reduce dimensionality, but to restructure the solution space for stable dynamical evolution, a key requirement for reliable, efficient neural surrogates in scientific applications.

</details>


### [21] [Minimally Invasive Steering of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30218)
*Taha Entesari,Jingyu Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MISVO 用局部 KL 几何/Fisher 二次型约束 pre-logit steering，推导序列级 KL 梯度的 Fisher 项与后缀 score-function 项，并据此优化位置特定干预；在 1B–14B 模型上多数设置取得最高平均奖励，同时保持接近 Best-of-N 的多样性与连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pre-logit steering 通过向冻结语言模型最终隐状态添加向量，使其适应测试时奖励。但无正则的奖励优化会显著改变输出分布并损害生成质量，因此需要一种对模型干预更小、能控制分布偏移的导向方法。

Method: 提出 MISVO：以诱导 token 分布的局部 KL 几何惩罚干预；构造 Fisher 二次型度量分布敏感性，并利用与冻结语言模型 head 的矩阵—向量乘积解析计算梯度。推导序列级 KL 梯度的精确分解，即解析 Fisher 项与后缀 score-function 项；证明在固定生成长度下后缀项关于导向幅度为二阶小量，且三种 Fisher 代理与完整 KL 梯度一阶一致。采用冻结参考代理优化位置特定干预，不更新模型参数。

Result: 在约 1B–14B 参数模型的偏好任务与代码生成任务上，MISVO 在 7 个模型—任务设置中的 6 个取得最高平均奖励，且多样性、连贯性得分接近 Best-of-N。

Conclusion: MISVO 通过在奖励优化中显式约束分布偏移，实现了对冻结语言模型的高效、微创式测试时导向，在不更新模型参数的前提下兼顾奖励提升与生成质量。

Abstract: Pre-logit steering adapts a frozen language model to a test-time reward by adding vectors to its final hidden states. Unregularized reward optimization can substantially alter the output distribution and degrade generation quality. We propose Minimally Invasive Steering Vector Optimization (MISVO), which penalizes interventions using the local KL geometry of the induced token distribution. The resulting Fisher quadratic measures distributional sensitivity and admits an analytic gradient computed through matrix--vector products with the frozen language-model head. We derive an exact decomposition of the sequence-level KL gradient into an analytic Fisher term and a suffix score-function term. For a fixed generation horizon, we show that the suffix term is second order in the steering magnitude and that three Fisher surrogates agree with the full KL gradient to first order. MISVO uses the frozen-reference surrogate to optimize position-specific interventions without updating model parameters. Across preference and code-generation tasks on models with approximately 1B--14B parameters, MISVO achieves the highest mean reward in six of seven model--task settings, with diversity and coherence scores close to those of Best-of-N.

</details>


### [22] [PoEM: Predicting RL Outcomes from Existing Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30226)
*Kimia Hamidieh,Giannis Daras,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PoEM 框架：给定新奖励函数时，不运行额外 RL，而是利用已在其他奖励上后训练好的模型来预测/近似目标 RL 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型的 RL 后训练计算开销大、过程不稳定，且每当奖励模型改变或需要组合多个奖励时都必须从头运行 RL，因此需要探索能否直接预测新奖励下的 RL 结果。

Method: 首先证明若新奖励可写为已有奖励的线性组合，则新策略在 log 空间中也表现为已有 log 策略的线性组合；进一步发现即使奖励之间非线性相关，不同奖励 RL 训练得到的 log 策略通常张成近似低秩子空间。基于此，仅利用奖励或基策略在样本上的输出即可估计组合权重，并据此设计算法：输入后训练模型与新奖励函数，近似目标 RL 策略，而无需执行任何额外 RL 训练。

Result: 在合成奖励与真实奖励上进行了实验验证，覆盖文本和图像两种模态。

Conclusion: RL 后训练结果在新奖励下可以被预测；PoEM 能在不重新运行 RL 的情况下近似目标策略，为降低 RL 后训练成本与提升可组合性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Foundation models are post-trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to maximize specific rewards, such as human alignment, correctness, or instruction following. This post-training process is computationally intensive, sometimes unstable, and has to be run from scratch every time the reward model changes or when we want to combine multiple rewards. We hence ask: given a new reward function, is it possible to predict the RL outcomes without actually running RL on it? We answer this in the affirmative by introducing PoEM, a framework to predict the outputs of RL on a new reward function using a set of models already post-trained on other rewards. First, we show that if the new reward function can be written as a linear combination of existing ones, then the new policy in log-space can be written as a linear combination of the existing log-policies. Surprisingly, even in cases where the rewards are not linearly connected, we observe that often log-policies from RL training span an approximately low-rank subspace across rewards. To our benefit, the weighting coefficients for this combination can be estimated using only the reward or basis policy outputs on the samples. We turn these observations into an algorithm that takes post-trained models and a new reward function, and approximates the target RL policy without actually running any additional RL training. We experimentally validate our approach across synthetic and real rewards, spanning both text and image modalities.

</details>


### [23] [To Trust or Not to Trust: Retrieval-Augmented Fact Checking in Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30227)
*Debajyoti Mazumder,Mamta,Abhirama Subramanyam Penamakuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 VeriSpeak——一个面向大音频语言模型（LALMs）的语音事实核查探针基准，包含 3,879 条口语声明，揭示出稳定的文本—语音模态差距，并表明检索需与显式推理结合才能有效验证口语声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线虚假信息日益以语音形式出现（新闻片段、播客、访谈、政治演讲、社交媒体视频），因此需要能够直接从语音中验证声明的事实核查系统；同时需探究事实核查能力能否从文本迁移到语音，以及检索增强的 LALM 能否利用文本证据支持或反驳口语声明。

Method: 构建 VeriSpeak 探针基准：包含 3,879 条口语声明，覆盖时间、地理与关系类事实，真假标签均衡。基准设计用于检验文本到语音的迁移性，并评估检索增强 LALM 利用文本证据验证口语声明的能力；实验对比了纯检索、检索+显式推理以及 thinking-tuned LALM 等设置。

Result: 实验发现一致的文本—语音模态差距：能可靠验证书面声明的 LALM 在相同声明转为语音后常常失败；仅靠检索增益有限，模型常将检索到的证据与口语声明混为一谈；而检索结合显式推理可改善声明—证据对比，thinking-tuned LALM 达到 86.1% 准确率。

Conclusion: 有效的语音虚假信息检测不仅需要语音理解能力，还需要对检索证据进行有依据（grounded）的推理。数据集已在 Hugging Face 公开。

Abstract: Online misinformation increasingly appears in spoken formats such as news clips, podcasts, interviews, political speeches, and social media videos, creating a need for fact-checking systems that can verify claims directly from speech. We introduce VeriSpeak, a probe benchmark for studying speech-based fact verification in Large Audio Language Models (LALMs). VeriSpeak contains 3,879 spoken claims spanning temporal, geographical, and relational facts, with balanced true and false labels. The benchmark is designed to examine whether factual verification ability transfers from text to speech, and whether retrieval-augmented LALMs can use textual evidence to correctly support or refute spoken claims. Our experiments reveal a consistent text-speech modality gap: LALMs that verify written claims reliably often fail on the same claims when spoken. Moreover, retrieval alone provides limited gains because models frequently conflate retrieved evidence with the spoken claim. In contrast, retrieval combined with explicit reasoning improves claim-evidence comparison, with a thinking-tuned LALM reaching 86.1% accuracy. VeriSpeak highlights that effective speech misinformation detection requires not only speech understanding, but also grounded reasoning over retrieved evidence. The dataset is publicly available via Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/abhiram4572/VeriSpeak.

</details>


### [24] [Temporal Gradient Inversion for Private Trajectory Reconstruction in Embodied Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30258)
*Sudip Bhujel,Shanghao Shi,Ruiquan Huang,Ning Zhang,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE：面向具身强化学习分布式训练的时序梯度反演攻击，利用连续梯度的跨时间相关性与策略头梯度的闭式动作恢复，自回归重建私有观测-动作轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式具身强化学习仅上传策略梯度、保留原始传感器数据，常被视为具备隐私保护；但已有单帧梯度反演攻击忽略了时序结构，连续策略学习梯度中的时间相关性可能显著放大隐私泄露。

Method: 提出TRACE（Temporal Reconstruction Attack on Consecutive Encodings），一种摊销式时序梯度反演攻击：从每步策略学习梯度中自回归重建私有观测-动作轨迹。利用两个结构信号：(i) 连续具身梯度间的跨时间相关性，并以条件互信息上界形式化；(ii) 从策略头梯度结构中闭式恢复动作，并证明在标准熵正则足够小时该恢复是精确的。

Result: 在留出的具身场景中，TRACE达到18.8 dB PSNR，动作恢复近乎完美，每帧重建耗时3–4.5 ms；在所有重建指标上优于基于学习的基线，并超过优化攻击且速度快若干数量级。进一步评估表明其可推广至循环、残差和紧凑Transformer受害架构、多模态输入及更大的离散动作空间。

Conclusion: 防御实验表明，保护时序梯度流可能需要序列感知的隐私机制；仅依赖单帧或逐帧防护不足以应对TRACE类时序梯度反演攻击。

Abstract: Distributed learning in embodied reinforcement-learning agents offers a degree of privacy by retaining raw sensor data on-device and transmitting only policy gradients to the server. Yet temporal structure can amplify this leakage beyond single-frame attacks. We introduce Temporal Reconstruction Attack on Consecutive Encodings (TRACE), an amortized temporal gradient-inversion attack that autoregressively reconstructs the sequence of private observation-action trajectories from per-step policy-learning gradients. The attack exploits two structural signals ignored by prior single-frame methods: (i) cross-time correlation between successive embodied gradients, which we formalize via a conditional mutual-information bound, and (ii) closed-form action recovery from policy-head gradient structure, which we prove exact when standard entropy regularization is sufficiently small. On held-out embodied scenes, TRACE reaches $18.8$ dB PSNR with near-perfect action recovery at $3$-$4.5$ ms per reconstructed frame, dominating the learning-based baseline across all reconstruction metrics and exceeding optimization attacks while running orders of magnitude faster. Further evaluation demonstrates TRACE's broader applicability across recurrent, residual, and compact transformer victim architectures, multi-modal inputs, and larger discrete action spaces. Defense experiments suggest that protecting temporal gradient streams may require sequence-aware privacy mechanisms.

</details>


### [25] [Graph, Loop, and Harness Engineering for Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering and Analytical Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29668)
*Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两个零信任智能体框架：Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering 与 Zero-Trust Agentic OLAP，用图工程、循环工程与智能体-挂具工程统一编排代码、数据、基础设施与运行时，并以证据门控实现可验证的端到端云数据工程与 OLAP 执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体虽在自动化数据工作流上进展迅速，但端到端云数据工程与分析执行需要跨代码、数据、基础设施和运行时环境的可靠协同；现有流程缺乏零信任验证、证据约束与受控恢复机制，难以保证完成、授权与生产晋升的可靠性。

Method: 提出两个零信任框架。其一，Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering 从自然语言任务生成、部署并验证完整云数据工程方案，完成条件依赖仓库、部署、运行时与策略证据。其二，Zero-Trust Agentic OLAP 将受治理的数据准备与可验证 OLAP 结合，仅在验证与证据绑定批准后允许生产晋升，并仅在满足同快照执行、精确结果等价、确定性 grounding 与反思后才释放分析答案。两者共享三类抽象：图工程（证据门控工作流结构）、循环工程（有界恢复）、智能体-挂具工程（零信任执行）。

Result: 在名义执行、受控故障、有界恢复与策略约束条件下对两个框架进行评估，测量指标包括验证完成、恢复能力、授权执行、生产晋升与可验证 OLAP 执行。

Conclusion: 该工作以零信任与证据门控为核心，将智能体数据工程和 OLAP 分析统一到可验证执行范式中，为跨代码、数据、基础设施与运行时的云数据工作流提供可靠协同与生产级晋升保障。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly automate data workflows, but end-to-end cloud data engineering and analytical execution require reliable coordination across code, data, infrastructure, and runtime environments. We present two zero-trust frameworks. Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering generates, deploys, and verifies complete cloud data-engineering solutions from natural-language tasks, with completion conditioned on repository, deployment, runtime, and policy evidence. Zero-Trust Agentic OLAP combines governed Data Preparation with verified Online Analytical Processing (OLAP), permitting production promotion only after validation and evidence-bound approval, and releasing analytical answers only after Same-Snapshot Execution, Exact Result Equivalence, deterministic grounding, and reflection. Both frameworks share three abstractions: graph engineering for evidence-gated workflow structure, loop engineering for bounded recovery, and agent-harness engineering for zero-trust execution. We evaluate both frameworks under nominal execution, controlled failures, bounded recovery, and policy-constrained conditions, measuring verified completion, recovery, authorization enforcement, production promotion, and verified OLAP execution.

</details>
